# Triplex scorecard v0.1

**Bare-metal scorecard.**

Source `090c189a8ca01aaf4486b298837a37660034aa09` · age label `1B` · started 2026-11-23T23:19:43.796Z

Assembled at renderer `6c4f9e4fe97d354bdcef5f1b212fcd8a97f15b23` from this campaign, run at source `090c189a8ca01aaf4486b298837a37660034aa09`. The renderer differs from the source only in: `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/int1/V0.1-STATE.md`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/PRODUCTION-CARD.md`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/adopted-card.ts`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/renderer.ts`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/run.ts`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/windows.test.ts`, `lab/experiments/EXP-DX1/v01/windows.ts`. Production tapes checked by platform regeneration: each cell's tape hashes to the binding made before its first offer and matches this platform's regeneration in every field except the floating-point `ms` and `gap` (tolerance 0.000001 ms and 1e-9 s). p6-50-loaded: 0 offsets differ (max 0.00e+0 ms), 0 gaps (max 0.00e+0 s); p6-235-loaded: 65 offsets differ (max 5.82e-11 ms), 1 gaps (max 4.55e-13 s); p6-394-loaded: 213 offsets differ (max 9.17e-10 ms), 1 gaps (max 9.09e-13 s); p6-620-loaded: 106 offsets differ (max 5.64e-11 ms), 23 gaps (max 5.68e-14 s); barrier6/campaign/p6-50-loaded: 0 offsets differ (max 0.00e+0 ms), 0 gaps (max 0.00e+0 s); barrier6/campaign/p6-235-loaded: 65 offsets differ (max 5.82e-11 ms), 1 gaps (max 4.55e-13 s); barrier6/campaign/p6-394-loaded: 213 offsets differ (max 9.17e-10 ms), 1 gaps (max 9.09e-13 s); barrier6/campaign/p6-620-loaded: 106 offsets differ (max 5.64e-11 ms), 23 gaps (max 5.68e-14 s).

Population ten deep accounts of 100,000 people in one SCALE1 database. [Held seal and run inputs](campaign/input.json).

Budget 224 minutes including cleanup, up to USD 4.07 per age. Headline zero/readers use rolling6. Barrier6 repeats P2 and P6 on the identical stack. The P1–P4 pair uses 12 minutes of matched offers. Profile server-32 (transactor 60 GiB cap, 32 GiB object cache; role caps 79 GiB) with read gateway JVM j21-g1, write gateway JVM j21-g1, headline transactor j25-zgc, and separate WAL. P2 and P6 offered rates 50, 235, 394, 620 rows/s (offered as one commit per subject's save) for 90 seconds per cell. Save tape: the production-shaped tape, shape S (one commit per subject's save, at the same saves as shape R). Credit selection rolling6. Starts from prepared PGDATA copies. File-level preparation, build and AGE1 provisioning are separate.

**Save tape:** the production-shaped tape, shape S (one commit per subject's save, at the same saves as shape R). P2 below counts commits; the production table gives rows and saves.

The headline ordered profile runs the full workload. Barrier6 repeats P2 and P6 on the same launch profile.

Rows are placed in a cell and a half by their scheduled tape offset. Rows scheduled within half a floating-point step of their cell's end have a due time equal to the end on the epoch clock; they are counted in the cell and the second half they were offered in. Such rows per scope: headline · 50/s · all loaded saves: 9 · headline · 235/s · all loaded saves: 9 · headline · 394/s · all loaded saves: 9 · headline · 620/s · all loaded saves: 9 · barrier6 control · 50/s · all loaded saves: 9 · barrier6 control · 235/s · all loaded saves: 9 · barrier6 control · 394/s · all loaded saves: 9 · barrier6 control · 620/s · all loaded saves: 9.

## Production-shaped neighbour saves

Neighbour accounts only (the quiet account is P6). Latencies are p50 / p99 ms from the scheduled offer to the durable acknowledgement; save completion runs from a save's first row offered to its last row acknowledged. Storage reads are SQL reads per commit from each acknowledgement, in the first 60 s / from 150 s. The per-subject ordered writer takes only commits of one to three facts that each replace a value; every other commit takes the general writer. Commits over 256 assertions carry the general identity. Every field of every production subject was read back at the cell's terminal cut and matched the acknowledged writes.
